### [1] [DMC-CF: Dynamic Multimodal CounterFactual QA benchmark for Causal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29339)
*Junzhe Zhang,Huixuan Zhang,Guirong Wang,Xingyao Zhang,Pei Liu,Lin Qu,Hu Wei,Xiaojun Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个大规模真实世界的多模态因果反事实推理基准（DMC-CF），包括静态和动态评估两个部分，发现当前多模态大语言模型在真实世界场景中的因果推理能力仍有很大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）是否真正理解现实世界的因果关系是一个关键研究问题。现有多模态因果推理数据集存在规模有限或使用非真实多模态内容（合成图像/视频、卡通等）的问题。

Method: 1）收集真实世界视频构建DMC-CF-Static基准数据集；2）提出动态图干预（DGI）框架，使用因果图表示因果事件；3）基于DMC-CF-Static构建动态评估基准DMC-CF-Dynamic以解决数据污染问题。

Result: 在包含静态和动态评估的整体DMC-CF基准上的实验结果表明，当前多模态大语言模型在真实世界场景中的多模态因果推理能力仍需大幅改进。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了更真实、更全面的多模态因果推理评估基准，揭示了当前MLLMs在理解现实世界因果关系方面的局限性，为未来模型改进指明了方向。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), models have demonstrated increasingly powerful multimodal capabilities. However, whether MLLMs trained through statistical learning can truly understand the causal relationships underlying the real world remains a key research question. In recent years, numerous multimodal causal reasoning datasets have been proposed. Nevertheless, these datasets are either limited in scale or constructed from synthetic images and videos, cartoon-based content, or other non-realistic multimodal sources. To address these limitations, we collect real-world videos and construct DMC-CF-Static, a large-scale benchmark for multimodal causal counterfactual reasoning. Furthermore, to mitigate issues such as data contamination in traditional static evaluation, we represent causal events using causal graphs and propose the Dynamic Graph Intervention (DGI) framework to build the dynamic evaluation benchmark DMC-CF-Dynamic from DMC-CF-Static. Experimental results on the overall DMC-CF, which includes both static and dynamic evaluation benchmarks, demonstrate that the multimodal causal reasoning capabilities of current multimodal large language models in real-world scenarios still require substantial improvement.

</details>


### [2] [Semantic and Visual Evidence for Efficient Long-Video Reasoning: A Solution for the HD-EPIC VQA Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29402)
*Yinsong Xu,Wei Jing,Liuxin Zhang,Wanjun Lv,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个统一框架，通过解耦长视频推理为语义证据和视觉证据两种互补形式，并采用查询条件的证据检索与集成过程，有效提升了MLLMs在长时自我中心视频理解任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在理解长时自我中心视频时面临两大挑战：有限的上下文长度和对细粒度视觉细节的定位不足。HD-EPIC基准测试表明，即使是强大的长上下文模型在多样化视频问答任务中也表现较差，亟需新的解决方案。

Method: 提出了一个统一框架，将长视频推理解耦为两种互补证据：语义证据通过从粗到细的提取管道捕获全局程序结构；视觉证据则以物体为中心，通过边界框和视觉嵌入保留细粒度定位。推理阶段采用查询条件的证据检索与集成过程，动态选择和整合两种信息源的相关信息。

Result: 在HD-EPIC-VQA挑战赛的多个任务类别中取得了具有竞争力的性能表现。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，显式地构建、检索和集成语义证据与视觉证据对于MLLMs有效理解长视频至关重要，为长视频理解领域提供了重要的技术见解。

Abstract: Understanding long-form egocentric videos remains challenging for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) due to limited context length and insufficient grounding of fine-grained visual details. The recently proposed HD-EPIC benchmark highlights these limitations: even strong long-context models achieve relatively low performance across diverse video question answering tasks. In this paper, we propose a unified framework that decouples long-video reasoning into two complementary forms of evidence: semantic evidence and visual evidence. Semantic evidence captures global procedural structure through a coarse-to-fine extraction pipeline, while object-centric visual evidence preserves fine-grained grounding through bounding boxes and visual embeddings. During inference, we formulate reasoning as a query-conditioned evidence retrieval and integration process, dynamically selecting relevant information from both sources. Our approach achieves competitive performance in the HD-EPIC-VQA Challenge across multiple task categories. More broadly, our results demonstrate that explicitly structuring, retrieving, and integrating semantic and visual evidence is critical for effective long-video understanding with MLLMs.

</details>


### [3] [Recovering Policy-Induced Errors: Benchmarking and Trajectory Synthesis for Robust GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29447)
*Tianpeng Bu,Xin Liu,Qihua Chen,Hao Jiang,Shurui Li,Hongtao Duan,Lu Jiang,Lulu Hu,Bin Yang,Minying Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了GUI-RobustEval评估框架和RoTS数据合成方法，通过提升GUI智能体的错误恢复能力，在OSWorld上达到了47.4%成功率的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前GUI智能体虽然在快速发展，但缺乏从自身错误中恢复的鲁棒性，这阻碍了其在真实场景中的部署。研究发现在评估和数据层面都存在明显差距。

Method: 提出了两个核心组件：
（1）GUI-RobustEval：包含1,216个可执行测试用例，系统性地评估广泛且现实的错误模式下的错误恢复能力；
（2）RoTS（鲁棒性驱动轨迹合成）：基于树结构的可扩展合成框架，主动发现多样化的错误模式并合成对应的恢复步骤，生成了80万条高质量训练数据。基于此数据微调了RoTS-7B和RoTS-32B两个模型。

Result: 两个模型在GUI-RobustEval和传统GUI基准测试上均展现出显著提升。RoTS-32B在OSWorld上达到了最先进性能，成功率为47.4%，All-Pass@4得分为33.8%。

Conclusion: 提升长时域错误恢复能力不仅增强了鲁棒性，还能提升整体性能表现。这证明了在GUI智能体训练中系统性地考虑错误恢复机制的重要性。

Abstract: While GUI agents have advanced rapidly, they often lack the robustness to recover from their own errors, hindering real-world deployment. To bridge this gap at both the evaluation and data levels, we introduce GUI-RobustEval and propose Robustness-driven Trajectory Synthesis. GUI-RobustEval contains $1,216$ executable test cases that systematically measure error recovery capabilities across a broad and realistic spectrum of error modes. At the data level, RoTS is a scalable synthesis framework that creates $800k$ high-quality data via a tree-based pipeline that proactively discovers diverse error modes and synthesizes corresponding recovery steps. Our two models, RoTS-7B and RoTS-32B, fine-tuned on our dataset, both demonstrate significant gains on GUI-RobustEval and traditional GUI benchmarks. Notably, RoTS-32B achieves state-of-the-art performance on OSWorld, with a $47.4\%$ success rate and a $33.8\%$ All-Pass@4 score, suggesting that improved long-horizon error recovery ability contributes to both robustness and overall performance. Our code is available at https://github.com/AlibabaResearch/RoTS.

</details>


### [4] [Comparative evaluation of photogrammetric reconstruction methods and 3D Gaussian Splatting for road surface roughness analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29452)
*Marouane Elmegdar,Teng Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究比较了四种3D重建流水线（COLMAP、Meshroom、Metashape和3D Gaussian Splatting）利用智能手机图像估算道路表面粗糙度的能力，证明开源方案能够实现低成本路面监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于传感器的道路表面评估技术成本较高，本研究旨在探索基于图像的3D重建技术作为一种低成本替代方案的可行性，特别是评估其在利用智能手机图像估算道路表面粗糙度方面的能力。

Method: 对比四种重建流水线（COLMAP、Meshroom、Metashape、3D Gaussian Splatting），所有点云均在CloudCompare中采用一致的工作流程进行处理：方向对齐、分割、法线估计，并在0.2、0.4和0.6模型单位的邻域半径下计算粗糙度。

Result: COLMAP对微观纹理具有最高敏感度；Meshroom提供平衡的重建效果，粗糙度变化适中；Metashape因其内部滤波机制产生最平滑的几何形状；3DGS能够捕捉可见不规则性，但噪声较高且密度较低。

Conclusion: 开源重建流水线能够有效地用于相对粗糙度评估，为低成本路面监测提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Image-based 3D reconstruction offers a low-cost alternative to traditional sensor-based techniques for road surface assessment. This study compares four reconstruction pipelines--COLMAP, Meshroom, Metashape, and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS)--to evaluate their ability to estimate road surface roughness from smartphone imagery. All point clouds were processed in CloudCompare using a consistent workflow involving orientation alignment, segmentation, normal estimation, and roughness computation at neighborhood radiuses of 0.2, 0.4, and 0.6 model units. The results show that COLMAP provides the highest sensitivity to micro-texture, while Meshroom yields balanced reconstructions with moderate roughness variation. Metashape produces the smoothest geometry due to its internal filtering, and 3DGS captures visible irregularities but exhibits higher noise and lower density. The comparison demonstrates that open-source pipelines are viable for relative roughness evaluation, offering a practical approach for low-cost pavement monitoring.

</details>


### [5] [Uni-RCM: Unified Reference-guided Cross-modal Mapping for Multi-Class Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29455)
*Yangchen Wu,Huiqiang Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Uni-RCM框架，通过引入可学习参考特征和离线残差量化器，实现了统一的多模态工业异常检测，在多类别设置下达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖分离模型处理不同产品类别，限制了实际可扩展性。当转向统一范式同时处理多样类别时，类别间干扰和特征流形混淆导致检测精度下降。

Method: 提出Uni-RCM框架，包含两个核心组件：1）参考引导块，通过引入可学习参考特征动态过滤类别特定噪声，捕获跨模态共性；2）离线残差量化器，通过多个级联码本刻画正常分布。

Result: 在MVTec-3D AD数据集上进行广泛评估，在挑战性的多类别设置下实现了图像级检测和像素级定位的SOTA性能。

Conclusion: Uni-RCM成功解决了统一多类别异常检测中的类别间干扰和特征混淆问题，在多模态工业异常检测任务上取得了领先性能，提升了方法的实用性。

Abstract: Multi-modal industrial anomaly detection typically relies on separate models for each product category, fundamentally limiting practical scalability. When shifting to a unified paradigm that handles diverse classes simultaneously, detection accuracy often degrades due to inter-class interference and feature manifold confusion. To overcome these challenges, we propose a Unified Reference guided Cross-modal Mapping framework, named Uni-RCM. At its core, we propose a reference guide block to dynamically filter out category-specific noise by introducing a learnable reference feature, which captures the commonalities across different modalities. Besides, an offline residual quantizer is proposed to characterize the normal distribution by multiple cascaded codebooks. Extensive evaluations on the MVTec-3D AD dataset demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance in the challenging multi-class setting and in terms of image-level detection and pixel-level localization.

</details>


### [6] [FedSmoothLoRA: Toward Smoother and Faster Convergence in Federated Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29460)
*Zehao Wang,Guanglei Yang,Yihan Zeng,Hang Xu,Hongzhi Zhang,Wangmeng Zuo,Chun-Mei Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了FedSmoothLoRA框架，通过引入Round-Matching矩阵和Gradient-Aligned矩阵来解决联邦学习中LoRA微调存在的更新空间受限、轮次间状态不匹配和客户端起始状态无关性问题，实现了更快更平滑的收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习与LoRA的直接结合存在三个关键问题：(1)有限的更新空间限制了模型的有效学习能力；(2)轮次间状态不匹配破坏了跨轮次本地优化的连续性；(3)客户端无关的起始状态减缓了本地收敛速度。现有方法虽然解决了更新空间问题，但后两个问题仍未得到充分解决。

Method: FedSmoothLoRA在每轮通信中通过两个矩阵构建本地LoRA初始化：Round-Matching矩阵用于保持跨轮次本地状态连续性，Gradient-Aligned矩阵通过本地数据上的梯度信号估计提供客户端特定的优化指导。这种设计保持了扩大的更新空间，改善了跨轮次本地优化连续性，并为本地训练提供了客户端感知的起始状态。

Result: 在图像分类和自然语言生成任务上的大量实验表明，FedSmoothLoRA在多个任务上始终优于现有的联邦LoRA微调方法，实现了更平滑、更快的收敛。

Conclusion: FedSmoothLoRA成功解决了联邦LoRA微调中的多个核心挑战，通过创新的双矩阵初始化策略有效提升了模型的优化效率和性能表现，为联邦学习场景下的大模型微调提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning of foundation models with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) provides an efficient solution for reducing communication and computation costs while preserving data locality. However, the direct combination of FedAvg and LoRA suffers from three key issues: limited update space, which restricts the model's effective learning capacity; inter-round state mismatch, which disrupts cross-round local optimization continuity; and a client-agnostic starting state, which slows local convergence on clients. Although recent methods mitigate the limited update space issue by merging LoRA updates into the backbone across communication rounds, inter-round state mismatch and the client-agnostic starting state remain insufficiently addressed. To address these issues, we propose FedSmoothLoRA, a federated LoRA tuning framework that preserves the enlarged update space, improves cross-round local optimization continuity, and provides a client-aware starting state for local training. At each communication round, FedSmoothLoRA constructs the local LoRA initialization using two matrices: a Round-Matching matrix that preserves cross-round local state continuity, and a Gradient-Aligned matrix that provides client-specific optimization guidance from gradient signals estimated on local data. Together, these designs enable smoother and faster convergence. Extensive experiments on image classification and natural language generation tasks demonstrate that FedSmoothLoRA consistently outperforms existing federated LoRA tuning methods. Code: https://github.com/wangzehao0704/FedSmoothLoRA

</details>


### [7] [FlowSeg: Dynamic Semantic Guidance for LLM-Conditioned Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29461)
*Zekang Zhang,Guangyu Gao,Youyun Tang,ChengJing Wu,Xiaochao Qu,Chi Harold Liu,Jianbo Jiao,Yunchao Wei,Luoqi Liu,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowSeg提出了双向语义流机制，在LLM条件分割中实现了语言条件与掩码生成过程的动态交互，解决了语义错位问题并达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的LLM条件分割方法（propose-then-select流程）存在一个持续失败模式：虽然能生成高质量掩码候选，但最终预测往往无法匹配给定的语言条件。这是因为语言语义通常仅作为静态提示或事后匹配信号使用，而未参与迭代掩码生成过程。作者通过系统分析发现，许多错误源于语义错位而非掩码质量差。

Method: 提出了FlowSeg方法，核心是引入动态语义引导机制：
1. 双向语义流：在中间解码状态与LLM导出的条件嵌入之间建立双向流动，贯穿整个生成过程
2. 语言条件在每个阶段主动引导掩码细化
3. 条件嵌入根据新出现的视觉证据逐步更新
4. 轻量级边界感知细化：选择性增强不确定区域而不干扰置信内部区域

Result: 在指代表达分割和推理分割任务上的大量实验表明，FlowSeg持续改善语言-掩码对齐，并达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 通过将语言条件从静态角色转变为积极参与掩码生成过程的动态角色，FlowSeg有效解决了语义错位问题，生成了语义扎根的掩码表示和视觉对齐的语言条件，实现了更可靠的匹配。

Abstract: LLM-conditioned segmentation has recently advanced rapidly by coupling large language models with iterative mask generation frameworks. However, we identify a persistent failure mode in current propose-then-select pipelines. Although high-quality mask candidates are often generated, the final prediction may fail to match the given linguistic condition. This failure arises because language semantics are typically used as static prompts or post-hoc matching signals, rather than participating in the iterative mask generation process. Through systematic analysis, we show that many errors stem from semantic misalignment rather than poor mask quality. To address this issue, we propose FlowSeg, which introduces dynamic semantic guidance via a bidirectional semantic flow between intermediate decoding states and LLM-derived condition embeddings throughout the generation process. Language conditions actively guide mask refinement at each stage, while condition embeddings are progressively updated by emerging visual evidence. This design yields semantically grounded mask representations and visually aligned language conditions, enabling more reliable matching. We further incorporate a lightweight boundary-aware refinement to selectively enhance uncertain regions without perturbing confident interiors. Extensive experiments on referring expression segmentation and reasoning segmentation tasks demonstrate that FlowSeg consistently improves language-mask alignment and achieves state-of-the-art performance. Project page: https://zkzhang98.github.io/FlowSeg_page

</details>


### [8] [Benchmarking Large Vision-Language Models on CFMME: A Comprehensive Chinese Financial Multimodal Evaluation Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29462)
*Qian Chen,Xianyin Zhang,Yanzhi Liu,Lifan Guo,Feng Chen,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了CFMME，一个针对大型视觉语言模型的中文金融多模态评测基准，包含6,052个实例，涵盖多种金融任务。评估结果显示当前最先进模型仍存在显著改进空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了全面评估大型视觉语言模型在中文金融业务全流程中的感知、理解、推理和认知能力，需要构建一个专门的评测基准。现有模型在金融领域的多模态任务表现需要系统性评估。

Method: 构建CFMME评测基准，包含6,052个实例，覆盖从基础学术知识到复杂实际应用的场景，涉及八种主要金融图像模态和四项核心多模态任务（包括问答、检测、识别和信息提取）。在此基础上对代表性大型视觉语言模型进行系统性评测，并分析错误原因、跨模态能力和多方向设置。

Result: 当前最先进模型在问答任务上总体准确率为66.11%，在检测、识别和信息提取任务上平均得分为77.18分。这表明现有大型视觉语言模型仍有巨大改进空间。通过详细分析发现了错误原因、跨模态能力和多方向设置等方面的问题。

Conclusion: 现有大型视觉语言模型在金融领域的多模态任务上表现有待提升。CFMME基准可以推动该领域研究进展，特别是在提升金融领域多模态任务性能方面提供有价值的见解和方向。

Abstract: The emergence of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) has substantially expanded model capabilities beyond text-only understanding, enabling unified inference across both visual and textual modalities and supporting a broader range of real-world applications. To comprehensively evaluate the perception, understanding, reasoning, and cognition capabilities of LVLMs throughout the entire financial business workflow in Chinese contexts, we introduce CFMME, a novel Chinese financial multimodal evaluation benchmark. CFMME comprises 6,052 instances spanning from fundamental academic knowledge to complex real-world applications, covering eight primary financial image modalities and four core multimodal tasks. On CFMME, we conduct a thorough evaluation of representative LVLMs. The results show that the state-of-the-art model attains an overall accuracy of 66.11\% on the question answering task and an average score of 77.18 on the detection, recognition, and information extraction tasks, indicating substantial room for improvement in current LVLMs. In addition, we conduct detailed analyses of error causes, cross-modal capabilities, and multi-orientation settings, yielding valuable insights for future research. We hope that CFMME will spur further progress in LVLMs, especially by improving their performance on multiple multimodal tasks in the financial domain.

</details>


### [9] [V2XCrafter: Learning to Generate Driving Scene Across Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29471)
*Yihang Tao,Yu Guo,Senkang Hu,Yanan Ma,Zihan Fang,Sam Kwong,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出V2XCrafter，首个用于生成跨智能体相机视图的可控且真实的协同驾驶场景框架，通过渐进式多智能体扩散模型和跨智能体注意力模块解决V2X协同感知中的数据稀缺和泛化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同驾驶系统利用V2X通信进行多智能体协同感知以提升驾驶安全性，但受限于稀缺的真实世界标注V2X驾驶数据集以及在不同驾驶条件下的有限泛化能力。现有的面向单车多视角场景的图像生成方法在多智能体驾驶设置中面临两个根本挑战：学习目标的扩展会降低生成质量，智能体之间的高度动态变化阻碍了联合观测对象的物理属性（如颜色、类别）一致性建模。

Method: 开发基于单智能体骨干的渐进式多智能体扩散模型，使用相邻智能体的潜在状态作为参考信号，逐步指导单智能体到多智能体的扩散过程。提出跨智能体注意力模块，利用协同视图图和可学习的联合观测对象表示来建模动态跨智能体相机视图关系，以解决跨车辆不一致性问题。

Result: 实验表明，V2XCrafter能够生成高保真且可控的街景视图，并在智能体之间保持一致性，从而有效增强下游协同3D目标检测任务的性能。

Conclusion: V2XCrafter成功填补了多智能体协同驾驶场景生成的空白，为V2X协同感知系统提供了一种有效的数据增强解决方案，在生成质量和跨智能体一致性方面取得了显著成效。

Abstract: Collaborative driving systems leverage vehicle-to-everything (V2X) communication for multi-agent collaborative perception to enhance driving safety, yet they remain constrained by scarce annotated real-world V2X driving datasets and limited generalization across diverse driving conditions. While image generation technology offers a feasible solution for data augmentation, existing methods tailored for single-vehicle multi-view scenarios face two fundamental challenges in multi-agent driving settings: (1) the expansion of the learning objective degrades generation quality, and (2) the highly dynamic variations across agents hinder the modeling of consistency for physical attributes (e.g., color, category) in jointly observed objects. To bridge this gap, we propose V2XCrafter, the first framework for generating controllable and realistic collaborative driving scene across agents' camera views. For effective learning, we develop a progressive multi-agent diffusion model based on a single-agent backbone, using neighboring agents' latent states as reference signals to progressively guide the single-to-multi diffusion. To address cross-vehicle inconsistency, we propose a cross-agent attention module that leverages a collaboration view graph and learnable jointly observed object representation to model the dynamic cross-agent camera view relationships. Experiments have shown that V2XCrafter can generate high-fidelity and controllable street views with consistency across agents, thereby effectively enhancing the downstream collaborative 3D object detection tasks.

</details>


### [10] [AnyMo: Scaling Any-Modality Conditional Motion Generation with Masked Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29488)
*Yiheng Li,Zhuo Li,Ruibing Hou,Yingjie Chen,Hong Chang,Hao Liu,Shiguang Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了大规模多模态运动数据集OmniHuMo及统一框架AnyMo，利用残差FSQ分词器与掩码建模Transformer，实现了任意模态组合下的高质量人体运动生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的条件化人体运动生成方法受限于固定的模态配置和特定任务架构，缺乏对跨模态交互及多模态缩放定律的探索，主要瓶颈在于大规模模态对齐运动数据的稀缺。

Method: 构建了包含超过5000小时运动数据、320万序列且具有精确多模态标注（文本、语音、音乐、轨迹）的OmniHuMo数据集；提出了AnyMo统一框架，结合了基于残差FSQ的运动分词器和可扩展的掩码建模Transformer，以支持任意模态组合的合成。

Result: 大量实验表明，AnyMo实现了高保真的运动合成，并在空间和风格属性的控制上表现出高度的灵活性。

Conclusion: 该工作通过提供大规模对齐数据集和统一生成框架，有效解决了多模态运动生成中的数据稀缺与架构限制问题，显著提升了生成的灵活性与质量。

Abstract: Conditional human motion generation remains a fundamental challenge in computer vision and robotics. Despite significant progress, current methods are often constrained by fixed modality configurations and task-specific architectures, leaving cross-modal interactions and the scaling laws of multimodal-conditioned synthesis largely underexplored. A key bottleneck is the scarcity of large-scale modality-aligned motion data, limiting generalization across diverse control signals. In this work, we introduce OmniHuMo, a large-scale, high-quality dataset comprising over 5,000 hours of motion and 3.2 million sequences with precisely aligned multimodal annotations (e.g., text, speech, music, and trajectory). Leveraging OmniHuMo, we propose AnyMo, a unified multimodal framework combining a Residual FSQ-based motion tokenizer with a scalable masked modeling transformer, enabling high-quality motion synthesis under arbitrary modality combinations. Extensive experiments show that AnyMo achieves high-fidelity synthesis while offering flexible control over both spatial and stylistic attributes.

</details>


### [11] [KGEdit: Ambiguity-Aware Knowledge Graphs for Training-Free Precise Video Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29509)
*Mingshu Cai,Miao Zhang,Chenghe Yang,Yixuan Li,Osamu Yoshie,Yuya Ieiri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出KGEdit框架，通过构建歧义感知知识图谱解耦提示词，并利用结构化语义注入模块和时序感知语义控制模块，提升文本到视频生成在复杂指令下的语义精确度与时间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的免训练视频生成方法在处理复杂文本指令时存在显著缺陷，具体表现为语义歧义、概念绑定错误以及跨帧不一致，限制了生成视频的质量和对指令的响应精度。

Method: 1. 构建歧义感知知识图谱（AAKG），将输入提示词解构为身份、关系、属性和负向约束四种结构化语义；2. 设计结构化语义注入模块（SSIM），将语义信号注入扩散Transformer的关键层以实现细粒度控制；3. 引入时序感知语义控制（TASC）模块，根据去噪过程的阶段特征动态调度语义目标。

Result: 实验结果表明，KGEdit在编辑精度和时间稳定性上均优于现有方法，同时在文本驱动的交互场景中展现了更高的效率和可控性。

Conclusion: KGEdit框架有效解决了复杂文本指令下的语义模糊和时序不一致问题，为文本到视频扩散模型提供了一种高效、精准的结构化语义控制方案。

Abstract: In recent years, training-free video generation has progressed remarkably. However, when handling complex textual instructions, existing methods still suffer from semantic ambiguity, incorrect concept binding, and cross-frame inconsistency. To address these issues, we propose KGEdit, a structured semantic control framework for text-to-video (T2V) diffusion models. Specifically, we first construct an ambiguity-aware knowledge graph (AAKG) to disentangle and disambiguate the input prompt, converting it into four types of structured semantics: identity, relation, attribute, and negative constraints. We then design a structured semantic injection module (SSIM) to inject these semantic signals into key layers of the diffusion Transformer, enabling fine-grained semantic control. In addition, we introduce a temporal-aware semantic control (TASC) module that dynamically schedules semantic objectives according to the stage-wise characteristics of the denoising process, further improving semantic alignment and temporal consistency. Experiments show that KGEdit outperforms existing methods in editing precision and temporal stability, while offering higher efficiency and controllability in text-driven interaction scenarios.

</details>


### [12] [GiPL: Generative augmented iterative Pseudo-Labeling for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29539)
*Jiacong Liu,Shu Luo,Yikai Qin,Yaze Zhao,Yongwei Jiang,Yixiong Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了GiPL框架，通过伪标签自训练和生成式数据增强两个分支来解决跨域少样本目标检测(CD-FSOD)中的支持集利用率不足和过拟合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言基础模型在CD-FSOD的微调过程中面临两大挑战：(1)由于稀疏的单实例标注导致支持集利用不足；(2)在极少量目标域样本下存在严重的过拟合问题。

Method: 提出GiPL双分支训练框架：第一分支采用迭代伪标签自训练范式，在支持集上进行零样本推理生成伪标注，与真实标签融合后迭代优化模型；第二分支引入基于大型视觉-语言模型的生成式数据增强流程，合成域对齐的多目标标注图像来丰富训练样本。

Result: 在三个CD-FSOD数据集(RUOD、CARPK、CarDD)的1/5/10-shot设置下进行广泛实验，GiPL持续显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: GiPL通过双分支设计有效解决了CD-FSOD任务中的关键挑战，充分利用支持集数据并抑制过拟合，在多个基准数据集上取得了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Vision-language foundation models have shown promising zero-shot generalization for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Object Detection (CD-FSOD). However, they face two critical challenges in fine-tuning: insufficient support set utilization due to sparse single-instance annotations, and severe overfitting under extremely limited target-domain samples. To address these issues, this paper proposes GiPL, an efficient two-branch training framework.In the first branch, we design an iterative pseudo-label self-training paradigm, which performs zero-shot inference on the support set to generate reliable pseudo-annotations, fuses them with ground-truth labels, and iteratively optimizes the model to fully exploit support set data. In the second branch, we introduce generative data augmentation pipeline using large vision-language models, which synthesizes domain-aligned, multi-object annotated images to enrich training samples and suppress overfitting. Extensive experiments on three challenging CD-FSOD datasets (RUOD, CARPK, CarDD) under 1/5/10-shot settings demonstrate that GiPL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods with significant performance gains.Code is available at \href{https://github.com/z-yaz/CDiscover}{CDiscover}.

</details>


### [13] [Learning Representations from 3D Gaussian Splats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29549)
*Julia Farganus,Krzysztof Żurawicki,Arkadiusz Gaweł,Weronika Jakubowska,Halina Kwaśnicka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统地评估了几何深度学习架构在三维高斯泼溅（3DGS）场景分类任务中的表现，揭示了不同架构家族的差异以及高斯特有属性对表示质量的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维高斯泼溅（3DGS）主要用于视图合成，但其在场景理解任务中的潜力尚未被充分探索。作者旨在填补这一空白，研究几何深度学习模型能否有效地处理3DGS表示以进行场景分类。

Method: 对点基和图基等多种几何深度学习架构进行比较评估；在传统点云数据集和专用高斯泼溅数据集上进行基准测试；将场景嵌入为潜在表示，并通过端到端分类、线性探测和聚类分析进行评估；分析不同几何感知架构和输入特征配置的有效性。

Result: 发现了不同架构家族之间存在一致性差异；揭示了高斯特有属性对表示质量的显著影响；明确了哪些架构更适合学习3DGS表示。

Conclusion: 研究证明不同几何深度学习架构在学习三维高斯泼溅表示方面效果各异，高斯特定属性对表示质量有显著影响，为3DGS在场景理解任务中的应用提供了重要见解。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is a recent approach for scene rendering. Although primarily designed for view synthesis, its potential for scene understanding tasks remains underexplored. In this work, we conduct a comparative evaluation of various geometric deep learning architectures for the classification of 3D scenes represented using Gaussian Splatting. We benchmark point-based and graph-based models across both traditional point cloud datasets and dedicated Gaussian Splatting datasets. Scenes are embedded into latent representations, which are evaluated through end-to-end classification, linear probing, and clustering analysis. Our study provides insight into the suitability of different geometry-aware architectures and input feature configurations for learning effective 3D Gaussian Splat representations. The results highlight consistent differences between architectural families and reveal the impact of Gaussian-specific attributes on the quality of representation.

</details>


### [14] [TAE: Target-aware enhancer for nighttime UAV tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29558)
*Yanyan Chen,Ruigang Fu,Yu Song,Ping Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对低光夜间UAV目标跟踪场景,提出了一种目标感知的低光增强框架(TAE),通过边界框弱监督实现区域感知增强,并构建了DarkSOT夜间跟踪基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光夜间条件下的严重图像退化阻碍了UAV全天候目标跟踪应用。现有图像增强方法难以区分目标和背景区域,容易导致背景噪声放大或目标特征受损。

Method: 提出目标感知低光增强框架(TAE):1)利用跟踪边界框的弱监督信号引导区域感知增强,确保操作聚焦目标区域;2)采用自适应RGB多曲线融合机制实现不同区域的精细建模与自适应调整;3)构建DarkSOT夜间UAV跟踪基准数据集,包含9个目标类别的268个序列。

Result: 在DarkSOT和UAVDark135数据集上的实验结果表明,TAE显著提升了低光夜间场景的跟踪性能,展现出强大的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: TAE框架有效解决了低光夜间UAV目标跟踪中的关键问题,为全天候跟踪应用提供了有效解决方案,DarkSOT数据集填补了该领域研究资源的空白。

Abstract: Severe image degradation under low-light nighttime conditions constitutes a core bottleneck preventing all-day applications for UAV-based single object tracking. Existing image enhancement methods often struggle to distinguish between target and background regions, which can easily lead to amplified background noise or compromise target features. To overcome this limitation, we propose TAE, a target-aware low-light enhancement framework tailored for nighttime object tracking. Guided explicitly by weak supervisory signals from tracking bounding boxes, the framework performs region-aware enhancement to ensure operations focus on the target area. It further adopts an adaptive RGB multi-curve fusion mechanism to achieve refined modeling and adaptive adjustment across different regions. To facilitate research in this domain, we also contribute DarkSOT, a new benchmark for nighttime UAV tracking, comprising 268 sequences across 9 target categories. Experimental results on the DarkSOT and UAVDark135 demonstrate that TAE significantly improves tracking performance in low-light nighttime scenarios, exhibiting strong robustness and generalization. The DarkSOT dataset is available at https://github.com/Fu0511/DarkSOT-Dataset.

</details>


### [15] [From General Vision to Reliable Traversability Estimation: Adapting Vision Foundation Models for Unstructured Outdoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29565)
*Ji-Hoon Hwang,Jisung Bae,Dong-Wook Kim,Yeonkyu Lee,Seung-Woo Seo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Vision-to-Traversability Adaptation（ViTA）框架，通过可学习的可通行性提示、透视多样化训练和几何知识蒸馏，将视觉基础模型（以SAM2为实例）适配至可通行性估计任务，在真实越野场景中实现了最先进的性能和强跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于视觉的非结构化户外环境可通行性估计方法面临三个根本性挑战：（1）视觉基础模型（VFMs）的任务无关设计；（2）可通行性标注的歧义性；（3）语义标签与物理安全性之间的差异。这些问题严重影响了系统的可靠性。

Method: ViTA框架包含三个核心组件：（1）通过可学习的可通行性提示注入任务特定知识，同时保持VFM的跨域泛化能力；（2）引入透视多样化训练（Perspective-Diversified Training），估计语义不确定性以抑制歧义边界处的置信预测；（3）训练期间蒸馏几何知识，使模型仅从RGB图像推理坡度和高程信息，最终将语义和几何输出融合为连续可通行性评分。

Result: 在包括挑战性真实越野数据集的多个域上进行评估，ViTA实现了最先进的IoU和Precision指标，显著降低了假阳性率，并展现出强大的跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: ViTA成功解决了可通行性估计中的三大根本挑战，通过创新性的提示学习、不确定性建模和几何知识蒸馏，实现了可靠的可通行性估计，为非结构化户外环境导航提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-based approaches have become the dominant paradigm for traversability estimation in unstructured outdoor environments, typically adapting vision foundation models (VFMs) via semantic segmentation supervision. However, this paradigm faces three fundamental challenges that undermine its reliability: the task-agnostic design of VFMs, the ambiguity of traversability annotations, and the discrepancy between semantic labels and physical safety. We propose Vision-to-Traversability Adaptation (ViTA), a framework that adapts VFMs for reliable traversability estimation, instantiated on SAM2. ViTA injects task-specific knowledge through learnable traversability prompts while preserving the VFM's cross-domain generalization. To handle annotation ambiguity, we introduce Perspective-Diversified Training, which estimates semantic uncertainty to suppress confident predictions at ambiguous boundaries. To bridge the semantic-traversability discrepancy, we distill geometric knowledge during training, enabling slope and elevation reasoning from RGB images alone at inference. The semantic and geometric outputs are fused into a continuous traversability score that reflects both semantic uncertainty and geometric risk. Evaluations across diverse domains, including challenging real-world off-road datasets, demonstrate that ViTA achieves state-of-the-art IoU and Precision with substantial false-positive reduction and strong cross-domain generalization.

</details>


### [16] [Optimizing Latent Representations for Robust Building Damage Assessment Onboard Earth Observation Satellites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29575)
*Thomas Goudemant,Benjamin Francesconi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于AI的星载建筑物损毁评估系统，通过在地面编码灾前图像潜在表示并与星上灾后图像直接对比，显著降低数据下行延迟并提升应急响应速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然灾害或战区应急响应亟需快速识别受损建筑。虽然地球观测卫星能提供大范围覆盖，但受限于数据下行带宽、地面处理流程及人工判读，关键信息的获取存在显著延迟，影响了决策的时效性。

Method: 开发了一种星载AI系统，用于直接在卫星上进行物体级建筑物定位与损毁分类。该方法将灾前图像在地面编码为紧凑的潜在表示并上传至卫星，与星上新采集的灾后图像进行比对。研究系统性地探索了孪生处理、交叉注意力机制、潜在空间压缩及鲁棒性数据增强等技术，以优化星上计算效率。

Result: 在xBD数据集上的实验表明，该系统在图像存在错位的情况下仍能保持可靠的鲁棒性。即使在强压缩条件下，系统性能下降极小，有效验证了其在减少下行数据量的同时保留了任务关键信息。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在卫星端直接进行数据处理（边缘计算）的可行性，通过源头处理大幅减少了数据下行需求并提升了系统响应能力，为灾害响应提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Rapid identification of damaged buildings after natural disasters or on war areas is crucial to support emergency response and prioritize interventions. Earth Observation constellations provide timely, large-scale coverage, but actionable information is often delayed by data downlink constraints, on-ground processing, and human interpretation. Reducing this latency is essential to improve decision-making responsiveness. In this work, we propose an original AI-based system that enables object-level building damage assessment (localization and damage classification) directly onboard satellites from pre-disaster and post-disaster highresolution optical imagery. Available pre-disaster images are encoded on ground into compact latent representations, transmitted to the satellite, and compared on-board with newly acquired post-event observations. Leveraging AI interpretation capabilities and increasing processing capabilities on-board satellites, the proposed design enables processing directly at the data source, reducing the amount of information to be downlinked while preserving task-relevant content and improving overall system responsivity. We explore the design space through a systematic benchmark of onboard-compatible variants, analyzing the impact of siamese processing, cross-attention, latent-space compression, and robustness-oriented data augmentation. Experiments on xBD dataset demonstrate reliable and robust damage assessment under misalignment, with minimal performance degradation under strong compression.

</details>


### [17] [Mitigating State Aliasing in Vision-Language-Action Models via Inverse Dynamics Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29577)
*Kyujin Lee,Injae Kim,Jihwan Park,Yejun Ju,Minseok Joo,Hyunwoo J. Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出逆动力学学习作为辅助目标来监督VLA视觉编码器，通过预测当前和未来观测之间的动作来缓解状态混叠问题，从而学习更具状态区分性的视觉表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型通过适应预训练视觉-语言模型(VLMs)进行动作预测，但VLM衍生的表征对低层控制所需的细微视觉差异不敏感，导致视觉相似但需要不同动作的状态之间发生状态混叠。先前的VLA研究通过生成视觉或推理输出来改进视觉理解，但这些目标仅通过端到端预测间接塑造视觉编码器，并未显式分析学习到的视觉特征空间中的状态混叠问题。

Method: 论文引入逆动力学学习作为辅助目标，直接监督VLA视觉编码器。通过预测当前和未来观测之间的动作，鼓励编码器捕获决定低层动作的细粒度视觉差异。进一步使用伪反转监督，使编码器接触更广泛的动作方向，在有限的机器人演示下提高泛化能力。该方法适用于多种VLA基线，仅使用标准的观测-动作对而无需额外标注，并在测试时保持原始推理流程。

Result: 在CALVIN ABC-D和SimplerEnv上的实验显示，该方法在多种VLA基线上均取得一致的性能提升。冻结编码器探测和状态特征对齐分析进一步表明，该方法学习到了具有状态区分性的视觉表征，减少了状态混叠并更好地与机器人状态变化对齐。

Conclusion: 逆动力学学习作为辅助目标能有效缓解VLA模型中的状态混叠问题，通过直接监督视觉编码器学习细粒度视觉表征，从而提升机器人在视觉相似但动作不同场景下的操控性能，且无需额外标注即可应用于多种VLA基线。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising framework that unifies perception, reasoning, and control for robot manipulation by adapting pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) to action prediction. However, VLM-derived representations are often insensitive to subtle visual distinctions required for low-level control, causing state aliasing between visually similar states that require substantially different actions. Prior VLA studies improve visual understanding by generating visual or reasoning outputs, such as future frames, 2D grounding points or traces, or intermediate spatial reasoning steps, but these objectives typically shape the vision encoder only indirectly through end-to-end prediction and do not explicitly analyze state aliasing in the learned visual feature space. To mitigate state aliasing, we introduce inverse dynamics learning as an auxiliary objective that directly supervises the VLA vision encoder. By predicting the action between current and future observations, our objective encourages the encoder to capture fine-grained visual distinctions that determine low-level actions. We further use pseudo-reversed supervision to expose the encoder to a broader range of action directions and improve generalization under limited robot demonstrations. Our method applies to diverse VLA baselines, uses only standard observation-action pairs without additional annotations, and preserves the original inference pipeline at test time. Experiments on CALVIN ABC-D and SimplerEnv show consistent gains across diverse VLA baselines. Frozen-encoder probing and state-feature alignment analyses further show that our method learns state-discriminative visual representations that reduce state aliasing and better align with robot state changes.

</details>


### [18] [ReactBench: A Cause-Driven Benchmark for Multimodal Hallucination via Systematic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29579)
*Shizhe Zhou,Bohan Jia,Kai Wu,Yan Shen,Tongyun Li,Yuyang Wu,Shaohui Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReactBench 是一个面向原因驱动的多模态幻觉基准测试，通过四种针对性任务系统性地暴露并诊断当前MLLMs在视觉语言理解中的幻觉根本原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在视觉语言理解方面取得了快速进展，但它们仍然容易产生多模态幻觉，即生成与视觉输入不一致的响应。现有基准主要关注检测幻觉结果，而非评估这些失败的根本原因。此外，许多基准依赖于简单场景和有限的评估格式，已无法有效挑战最先进的模型。

Method: 提出ReactBench基准，采用原因驱动的幻觉评估方法：
1) 生成对抗性图像和幻觉诱导查询
2) 设计四种针对性任务：关系擦除、反事实属性、变更追踪和密集计数
3) 采用考试式评估格式
4) 利用思维链推理识别每个任务中的细粒度幻觉子原因
这些任务系统性揭示了共现偏差、语言先验、跨图像比较感知缺陷和细粒度感知瓶颈。

Result: 广泛评估表明，当前MLLMs对特定原因的幻觉触发因素仍表现出显著脆弱性，验证了ReactBench作为诊断工具的有效性。

Conclusion: ReactBench为多模态模型鲁棒性的诊断和改进提供了一个系统且可解释的测试平台，有助于深入理解和解决多模态幻觉问题。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved rapid progress in vision-language understanding, they remain prone to multimodal hallucinations, producing responses that are inconsistent with the visual input. Existing benchmarks predominantly focus on detecting hallucination outcomes rather than evaluating the underlying causes of these failures. Moreover, many benchmarks rely on simplistic scenarios and limited evaluation formats that no longer challenge state-of-the-art models. To address these limitations, we introduce ReactBench, a cause-driven hallucination benchmark featuring multiple tasks and an exam-style evaluation format. By generating adversarial images and hallucination-inducing queries, ReactBench introduces four targeted tasks: Relational Erasure, Counterfactual Attribute, Alteration Tracing, and Dense Counting. These tasks systematically expose co-occurrence bias, language priors, cross-image comparative perception deficiencies, and fine-grained perceptual bottlenecks. Beyond standard accuracy-based evaluation, we leverage Chain-of-Thought reasoning to identify fine-grained sub-causes of hallucination within each task. Extensive evaluations reveal that current MLLMs remain notably vulnerable to cause-specific hallucination triggers, demonstrating the value of ReactBench as a systematic and interpretable testbed for diagnosing and improving multimodal model robustness. The project page is available at https://reactbench.github.io/.

</details>


### [19] [BitC-3DGS: High-Capacity 3D Gaussian Splatting Watermarking via Bit Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29583)
*Yuquan Bi,Baosheng Yu,Yingke Lei,Jianwei Yang,Hongsong Wang,Jie Gui,Yuan Yan Tang,James Tin-Yau Kwok*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了BitC-3DGS框架，通过位压缩token化方案突破CLIP的77-token限制，在3D Gaussian Splatting资产中实现高容量水印，支持128-bit消息容量，性能达到与现有64-bit方法相当的恢复准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D Gaussian Splatting资产需要高容量水印来嵌入丰富的信息（如所有权、来源和认证码），以实现大规模3D资产流水线中的可靠识别和完整性验证。然而，现有基于预训练文本编码器的bit-to-token水印方法受限于CLIP固定的77-token上下文长度，最多只能嵌入77-bit消息，因为超出此限制的token不受学习位置嵌入的支持。

Method: 提出BitC-3DGS位压缩框架，包含三个核心组件：（1）位压缩token化方案，将同一chunk中的多个位编码为单个语义token；（2）双分支架构，用于联合chunk解压缩和位解码以恢复压缩信息；（3）hard-message采样策略，改善解码器训练期间的组合覆盖能力。

Result: 在Blender和LLFF数据集上进行了大量实验，证明了BitC-3DGS在高容量水印方面的有效性。该框架支持128-bit消息容量，其恢复准确率与最新SOTA方法的64-bit消息相当，同时保持高渲染保真度。

Conclusion: BitC-3DGS成功突破了现有方法的容量限制，在保持高恢复准确率和渲染质量的前提下，实现了高容量的3D资产水印嵌入，为大规模3D资产管道中的版权保护和完整性验证提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: High-capacity watermarking is necessary for 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) assets to embed rich information (e.g., ownership, provenance, and authentication codes), enabling reliable identification and integrity verification in large-scale 3D asset pipelines. Existing bit-to-token watermarking methods based on a pre-trained text encoder are limited to 77-bit messages due to CLIP's fixed 77-token context length, as tokens beyond this limit are unsupported by learned positional embeddings. To address this limitation, we introduce BitC-3DGS, a bit-compression framework that encodes multiple message bits per token. It employs a bit-compressed tokenization scheme that encodes multiple bits within the same chunk into a single semantic token. To enable recovery of the compressed information, it further introduces a dual-branch architecture for joint chunk decompression and bit decoding, along with a hard-message sampling strategy to improve combinatorial coverage during decoder training. Extensive experiments on the Blender and LLFF datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of BitC-3DGS for high-capacity watermarking, achieving high message recovery accuracy and rendering fidelity. For example, it supports 128-bit message capacity with recovery accuracy comparable to that of 64-bit messages in recent state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [20] [Brain-IT-VQA: From Brain Signals to Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29588)
*Roman Beliy,Matias Cosarinsky,Oliver Heinimann,Navve Wasserman,Michal Irani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了Brain-IT-VQA框架用于从fMRI信号进行视觉问答，并构建了NSD-VQA数据集和基准，在性能上大幅超越现有方法，同时为研究大脑视觉表征提供了有效工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从fMRI信号解码视觉内容并回答关于所见图像的问题是一项长期挑战。尽管近年来在基于fMRI的视觉问答(VQA)方面取得了显著进展，但性能仍然有限。此外，虽然最近的模型预测准确性不断提高，但很少被用作理解大脑视觉表征结构的工具。

Method: 研究提出了Brain-IT-VQA框架，基于Brain Interaction Transformer (Brain-IT)，从大脑活动中解码语言标记，并将其与语言模型集成以回答视觉问题。同时引入了NSD-VQA数据集和基准，平均每张图像提供20个问答对，跨越20个受控问题类别，解耦多个层次的视觉理解，使得在有限的fMRI测试数据下能够进行更可靠和可解释的评估。

Result: 该模型大幅超越了以往的基于fMRI的图像描述和VQA方法。利用新基准，研究者量化了哪些形式的视觉和语义信息可以从对自然图像的fMRI响应中可靠地解码，并分析了不同脑区在不同问题类型中的贡献。

Conclusion: Brain-IT-VQA和NSD-VQA共同提供了一个强大的预测框架和研究大脑表征的工具，不仅实现了优异的预测性能，还为理解视觉信息在大脑中的表征提供了新的研究手段。

Abstract: Decoding visual content from fMRI signals recorded while a person views images, and specifically answering questions about the seen images, is a long-standing challenge. While significant progress has been made in recent years in visual question answering (VQA) from fMRI, performance remains limited. Moreover, although recent models can make increasingly accurate predictions, they have rarely been used as tools for understanding the structure of visual representations in the brain. We present Brain-IT-VQA, a framework for visual question answering from fMRI. Building on the Brain Interaction Transformer (Brain-IT), our method decodes language tokens from brain activity and integrates them with a language model to answer visual questions. Our model substantially outperforms previous fMRI-based captioning and VQA approaches. We further introduce NSD-VQA, a new dataset and benchmark for visual question answering from fMRI. Unlike existing image-fMRI VQA datasets, which typically provide only a few broad and weakly controlled questions per image, NSD-VQA provides on average 20 question-answer pairs per image across 20 controlled question categories that disentangle multiple levels of visual understanding. This enables more reliable and interpretable evaluation despite limited fMRI test data. Together, Brain-IT-VQA and NSD-VQA provide both a strong predictive framework and a tool for studying brain representations. Using this benchmark, we quantify which forms of visual and semantic information can be reliably decoded from fMRI responses to natural images. We further analyze the contributions of different brain regions across question types.

</details>


### [21] [Non-Forgetting Knowledge Allocation with Bi-level Competition for Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29592)
*Xiang Tan,Run He,Yawen Cui,Mengchen Zhao,Yan Wu,Tianyi Chen,Huiping Zhuang,Xiaonan Luo,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出NoFA-BC方法，通过构建非遗忘分配器和双层竞争机制，解决基于预训练模型的类增量学习中适配器分配不当和遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于预训练模型（PTMs）的类增量学习（CIL）方法使用任务特定适配器时，存在两个关键问题：1）在推理时采用统一的知识分配机制，忽略了任务差异的本质特性，导致适配器利用率次优；2）在CIL约束下，分配器随着任务演进容易出现遗忘现象。

Method: 提出非遗忘分配的双层竞争方法（NoFA-BC），包含三个核心组件：1）非遗忘分配器（NFA）：将分配器训练转化为递归最小二乘问题，实现与使用全部数据训练等价的分配器；2）双层竞争机制（BLC）：包括任务内Winner-Takes-All（WTA）机制提取任务内最显著logit表示适配器贡献，以及任务间Last-Ones-Fall（LOF）淘汰机制抑制无关适配器；3）稳定性增强（SE）过程进一步改善旧任务性能。

Result: 该方法实现了更好的适配器知识分配，能够为每个输入定制适配器的参与比例，同时通过稳定性增强过程提升了旧任务性能。

Conclusion: NoFA-BC有效解决了基于预训练模型的类增量学习中的适配器分配优化和灾难性遗忘问题，为PTMs在增量学习场景中的应用提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Class-Incremental Learning (CIL) with pre-trained models (PTMs) aims to sequentially adapt PTMs to new categories without forgetting old knowledge. Built upon PTMs, existing adapter-based methods mainly train models via distinct task-specific adapters, and present a uniform knowledge allocation for each adapter during inference. However, this allocation mechanism ignores the nature of task discrepancy and leads to suboptimal utilization of adapters. Also, under CIL constraint, an allocator is prone to forgetting when tasks evolve. To address these issues, we propose a Non-Forgetting Allocation with Bi-Level Competition (NoFA-BC). NoFA-BC constructs a non-forgetting allocator (NFA) by transforming the allocator training into a recursive least-squares problem and achieves an allocator equivalent to that trained with all data. Based on the NFA, a Bi-Level Competition (BLC) including an intra-task level Winner-Takes-All (WTA) mechanism and inter-task Last-Ones-Fall (LOF) elimination is proposed to provide better allocation of adapter knowledge. WTA extracts the most significant logit within a task to represent the adapter's contribution and LOF suppresses the irrelevant adapters. With BLC, participation ratio of each adapter can be tailored for each input. Moreover, a Stability Enhancement (SE) process is incorporated to further improve the performance of old tasks.

</details>


### [22] [CogniVerse: Revolutionizing Multi-Modal Retrieval-Augmented Generation with Cognitive Reflection and Geometric Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29602)
*Xiang Fang,Wanlong Fang,Changshuo Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了CogniVerse框架，通过认知启发的数学严谨方法解决多模态检索增强生成(MMRAG)中的关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MMRAG框架存在四方面局限：噪声和无关检索、跨模态语义错位、缺乏自适应推理、局部与全局上下文生成不连贯。

Method: 提出三个协同模块：(1)认知反思模块，动态评估检索必要性并过滤相关多模态内容；(2)多模态检索模块，利用信息几何在黎曼流形上对齐嵌入，并通过谱图理论优化知识图谱；(3)分层生成模块，采用最优传输损失平衡token级准确性与全局语义一致性。

Result: 在准确性和连贯性上显著优于现有最先进系统，同时降低了检索延迟。

Conclusion: 认知启发且数学严谨的方法能有效解决MMRAG的核心挑战，实现更精确、连贯的多模态知识问答。

Abstract: Multi-modal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (MMRAG) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for enhancing Multimodal Large Language Models in knowledge-intensive question answering by integrating external visual, textual, and structural knowledge. However, existing MMRAG frameworks suffer from critical limitations, including noisy and irrelevant retrieval, cross-modal semantic misalignment, lack of adaptive reasoning, and incoherent generation across local and global contexts. We introduce \textbf{CogniVerse}, a novel MMRAG framework that addresses these challenges through a cognitive-inspired, mathematically rigorous approach. Drawing from human-like reasoning, CogniVerse integrates three synergistic components: (1) a Cognitive Reflection Module that dynamically assesses retrieval necessity and filters relevant multi-modal content, reducing noise and computational overhead; (2) a Multi-modal Retrieval Module that aligns embeddings in a Riemannian manifold using information geometry and refines knowledge graphs via spectral graph theory, ensuring precise and coherent retrieval; and (3) a Hierarchical Generation Module that employs an optimal transport-based loss to balance token-level accuracy and global semantic coherence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CogniVerse significantly outperforms state-of-the-art systems in both accuracy and coherence, while reducing retrieval latency.

</details>


### [23] [Learning Context-Conditioned Predicate Semantics via Prototype Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29610)
*NamGyu Jung,Chang Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AlignG方法，通过原型反馈学习上下文条件的谓词语义，解决场景图生成中多义谓词的建模问题，在VG-150和GQA-200数据集上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场景图生成中，多义谓词（polysemous predicates）在不同上下文中具有不同含义，这是核心挑战。现有方法通过分解谓词为多个静态原型或检索相似样例来解决，但这些策略保持谓词表示静态，无法根据图像特定证据重组语义，导致在模糊上下文中产生系统性混淆。

Method: AlignG通过原型反馈学习上下文条件的谓词语义：(1)从每张图像内的关系候选推断上下文条件的谓词语义；(2)将适应后的语义反馈以重新校准关系表示；(3)学习目标将适应过程锚定到全局语义中心，在防止语义漂移的同时，当场景提供一致的关系线索时允许选择性重组。

Result: 在VG-150和GQA-200数据集上的实验表明：(1)相比最先进基线方法有一致性提升；(2)在SGDet设置下，VG-150的F@100提升+1.4；(3)GQA-200的F@100提升+2.7；(4)可视化显示原型根据场景证据选择性合并或分离谓词，呈现连贯的上下文依赖重组。

Conclusion: AlignG成功解决了多义谓词建模问题，通过允许动态的上下文依赖语义重组同时保持全局语义一致性，在标准基准测试上取得持续改进，并提供了可解释的语义适应过程可视化。

Abstract: In scene graph generation, a central challenge is modeling polysemous predicates whose meanings shift across contexts. Prior approaches address this issue by decomposing predicates into multiple static prototypes or retrieving semantically similar exemplars. However, these strategies keep predicate representations static and cannot reorganize semantics to reflect image-specific evidence, leading to systematic confusions in ambiguous contexts. We propose AlignG, which learns context-conditioned predicate semantics via prototype feedback. AlignG infers context-conditioned predicate semantics from the relation candidates within each image and feeds the adapted semantics back to recalibrate relation representations. The learning objective anchors this adaptation to global semantic centers, preventing semantic drift while still allowing selective reorganization when the scene provides consistent relational cues. Experiments on VG-150 and GQA-200 show consistent improvements over state-of-the-art baselines, with F@100 improvements of +1.4 on VG-150 and +2.7 on GQA-200 under SGDet. We further visualize per-image prototype similarity shifts and observe coherent context-dependent reorganization where prototypes selectively merge or separate predicates according to scene evidence. The code is available at https://github.com/Namgyu97/AlignG-SGG.pytorch.

</details>


### [24] [DiffSpot: Can VLMs Spot Fine-Grained Visual Differences in Web Interfaces?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29615)
*Linhao Zhang,Aiwei Liu,Yuan Liu,Xiao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了DiffSpot基准测试，用于评估视觉语言模型在网页界面中检测细微视觉差异的能力。测试结果显示，即使是最好的模型也仅能识别40.7%的真实变化，表明VLMs在精细视觉感知方面存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在高层次图像-文本对齐方面取得了进展，但在感知细微视觉差异方面能力有限。研究网页界面中的局部视觉变化不仅可以作为精细感知的诊断测试，也是GUI智能体和设计工具的实际需求。

Method: 提出DiffSpot代码驱动的基准测试：通过变异自包含HTML中目标元素的单个CSS属性来构建受控图像对，重新渲染页面并记录变化的属性、元素和变异幅度；使用接地门控机制确保渲染像素差异仅限于目标元素；基准包含4,400对图像（3,900对有差异，500对无差异用于幻觉控制）。

Result: 对13个前沿VLMs进行零样本评估发现：最佳模型仅识别40.7%的真实变化；所有模型在困难层的召回率均低于23%；难度强烈依赖于属性类型；像素幅度和CLIP距离都不能可靠预测召回率。

Conclusion: DiffSpot基准揭示了VLMs在网页界面细微视觉差异检测方面的重大局限性，突显了精细视觉感知仍是当前模型的关键弱点。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have made strong progress on high-level image-text alignment, yet their ability to perceive subtle visual differences remains limited. We study this problem in rendered web interfaces, where localized visual changes are both a diagnostic test of fine-grained perception and a practical requirement for GUI agents and design tools. We introduce \textbf{DiffSpot}, a code-driven benchmark for open-ended spot-the-difference on web interfaces. DiffSpot constructs controlled image pairs by mutating a single CSS property of a target element in self-contained HTML, re-rendering the page, and recording the changed property, element, and mutation magnitude. A grounding gate retains only pairs whose rendered pixel difference is confined to the target element. The benchmark contains 4{,}400 pairs, including 3{,}900 has-diff pairs balanced across 13 CSS-property operators and three difficulty tiers, plus 500 no-diff pairs for hallucination control. Evaluating 13 frontier VLMs zero-shot, we find that even the best model identifies only $40.7\%$ of true changes, with Hard-tier Recall below $23\%$ for every model. DiffSpot further shows that difficulty is strongly property-dependent: across CSS operators, neither pixel magnitude nor CLIP distance reliably predicts Recall.

</details>


### [25] [AgentCVR: Active Multi-Agent Cross-Video Reasoning via Script-Simulated Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29643)
*Yilun Qiu,Jiahe Wang,Cilin Yan,Jiayin Cai,Xiaolong Jiang,Yao Hu,Chun Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentCVR，一个多智能体框架，通过主控智能体协调视觉和音频智能体进行目标导向的证据提取，并采用脚本模拟强化学习进行高效训练，在跨视频推理任务上超越了单通道基线方法，达到了与最先进闭源系统相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨视频推理（CVR）是多模态智能的关键前沿问题，需要模型检索、对齐和聚合分布在不同视频中的证据。当前多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在CVR任务中表现不佳，因为简单的单通道策略将多个视频编码为共享的压缩上下文，可能会遮蔽罕见但关键的证据信息。

Method: 提出AgentCVR多智能体框架，将CVR视为主动证据获取任务。该框架采用主控智能体（Master Agent）迭代协调专门的视觉智能体和音频智能体进行目标证据提取。引入脚本模拟强化学习（Script-Simulated RL）方法，利用LLM生成的语义脚本和轻量级文本模拟器优化智能体策略，避免了在线探索中代价高昂的多模态推理过程。

Result: 在全面的CVR基准测试中，AgentCVR在复杂的跨视频对齐和定位任务上表现优异：超越了单通道基线方法，并达到了与最先进的闭源系统相当的性能水平。

Conclusion: 多智能体迭代协调方法结合脚本模拟强化学习为跨视频推理提供了有效解决方案，在保持可复现性（代码已开源）的同时，实现了与顶级闭源系统相当的性能表现，证明了主动证据获取策略的有效性。

Abstract: Cross-Video Reasoning (CVR) has emerged as a critical frontier in multimodal intelligence, requiring models to retrieve, align, and aggregate evidence distributed across multiple videos. Current Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) often struggle with CVR, as simple single-pass strategies encode multiple videos into a shared compressed context, potentially obscuring rare but critical evidence. In this paper, we propose AgentCVR, a multi-agent framework that treats CVR as an active evidence-acquisition task. AgentCVR employs a Master Agent to iteratively coordinate specialized Visual and Audio Agents for targeted evidence extraction. To ensure efficient training, we introduce Script-Simulated RL, which optimizes the agent's policy with LLM-generated semantic scripts and a lightweight text-based simulator, bypassing costly multimodal inference during online exploration. Experimental results on a comprehensive CVR benchmark show that AgentCVR outperforms single-pass baselines and achieves comparable performance to state-of-the-art closed-source systems, particularly in complex cross-video alignment and localization. To ensure reproducibility, our code is available at https://github.com/wang-jh24/AgentCVR.

</details>


### [26] [MARTIAN: A Rendering Framework for Aerial Mars Imagery from HiRISE Orbital Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29647)
*Dario Pisanti,Georgios Georgakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出了MARTIAN，一个基于Blender的开源渲染框架，利用真实的HiRISE轨道地图产品生成火星地形的逼真航空视图，为火星旋翼飞行器的视觉导航提供大规模标注数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 火星航空导航需要能够适应多样化光照条件和地形形态的视觉方法，但训练和评估这些方法面临关键瓶颈：缺乏大规模、标注的航空数据集。

Method: 开发了MARTIAN框架，该框架基于Blender渲染引擎，利用真实的HiRISE轨道地图产品合成火星地形的逼真航空视图。框架支持可控光照条件和可变高度，并生成具有精确位姿标注的观测数据。

Result: 该框架通过在Ingenuity和未来火星旋翼飞行器的地图定位系统中的实际部署得到验证，基于合成数据训练的深度图像匹配器成功在真实火星图像上进行了评估。

Conclusion: MARTIAN有效解决了火星航空导航数据稀缺问题，为视觉导航方法提供了公开可用的训练数据生成工具，并已在实际火星任务中得到成功应用。

Abstract: Aerial navigation on Mars requires vision-based pipelines that are robust to the diverse illumination conditions and terrain morphology of the Martian surface. A key bottleneck for training and evaluating such methods is the scarcity of large-scale, annotated aerial datasets. We present MARTIAN, an open-source Blender-based rendering framework that leverages real HiRISE orbital map products to synthesize realistic aerial views of the Martian terrain under controllable lighting conditions and at varying altitudes. MARTIAN generates observations with accurate pose annotations, directly addressing the scarcity of training data for vision-based navigation on Mars. The framework has been validated through its deployment in concurrent work on map-based localization systems for Ingenuity and future Mars rotorcraft, where synthetically trained deep image matchers were successfully evaluated on real Mars imagery. MARTIAN is publicly available at: https://github.com/nasa-jpl/martian.

</details>


### [27] [SuperVoxelGPT: Adaptive and Ordered 3D Tokenization for Autoregressive Shape Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29655)
*Yuan Li,Congyi Zhang,Xifeng Gao,Xiaohu Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SuperVoxelGPT框架，通过自适应且确定有序的超体素标记化方法，解决了自回归多模态大语言模型在3D生成中的标记化难题，实现了序列长度压缩至12.8%的同时达到最优生成质量和10倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归多模态大语言模型在3D生成中面临两难困境：基于集合的紧凑表示丢失空间顺序导致序列预测模糊，而均匀或八叉树体素网格虽保持顺序但产生严重冗余和过长序列，这种结构性权衡限制了稳定高效的自回归3D生成。

Method: 提出SuperVoxelGPT框架，首先预测粗粒度几何显著性分布，使用显著性引导的质心Voronoi划分构建形状自适应超体素分区（复杂区域分配细粒度单元，平滑区域分配大单元）；然后引入SuperVoxelVAE，基于文本和有序超体素布局微调预训练MLLM以自回归生成超体素标记。

Result: 在Trellis-500K数据集上的实验表明，SuperVoxelGPT将标记序列长度降至均匀体素标记化的12.8%，同时实现了最先进的生成质量，相比先前方法平均加速10倍。

Conclusion: 通过自适应且确定有序的超体素标记化，SuperVoxelGPT成功解决了自回归3D生成中空间顺序与序列效率之间的根本矛盾，为高质量高分辨率3D形状生成提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive multimodal large language models (MLLMs) enable 3D generation but struggle to scale to high-resolution shapes due to
  inadequate 3D tokenizations. Compact set-based representations discard deterministic spatial ordering, leading to ambiguous sequence
  prediction, while uniform or octree-based voxel grids preserve ordering at the cost of severe redundancy and excessively long
  sequences. This structural trade-off limits stable and efficient autoregressive 3D generation.
  We present SuperVoxelGPT, a representation-first framework that resolves this tension through adaptive and deterministically ordered
  supervoxel tokenization. Given a prompt, we first predict a coarse geometric saliency distribution and construct a shape-adaptive
  supervoxel partition using saliency-guided centroidal Voronoi tessellation, allocating fine-grained cells to complex regions and
  larger cells to smooth regions. Conditioned on the text and ordered supervoxel layout, we introduce a SuperVoxelVAE and fine-tune a
  pretrained MLLM to autoregressively generate supervoxel tokens.
  Experiments on Trellis-500K show that SuperVoxelGPT reduces token sequence length to 12.8% of uniform voxel tokenization while
  achieving state-of-the-art generation quality and an average 10$\times$ speedup over prior methods.

</details>


### [28] [OccamToken: Efficient VLM Inference with Training-Free and Budget-Adaptive Token Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29657)
*Geng Li,Guohao Chen,Ting Chen,Shilin Shan,Kuangji Zuo,Bofan Lyu,Tuo An,Gen Li,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的框架OccamToken，通过基于寄存器的相对证据测试替代传统的绝对排序剪枝，实现了视觉语言模型中视觉Token的高效压缩，在极低保留率（1.4%）下仍能保持超过93%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLMs）在预填充阶段因长视觉Token序列导致计算和内存开销巨大。现有的剪枝方法多采用绝对排序范式，但这种方法存在固有缺陷：注意力汇聚点扭曲了Token重要性排名，且图像冗余度和查询相关的视觉证据差异使得固定的Token预算在不同输入间不可靠。

Method: 提出OccamToken框架，一种无需训练的方法，用寄存器锚定的相对证据测试取代绝对Token排序。利用寄存器Token自然吸收低信息注意力模式的特性，将其作为识别真正信息性视觉证据的稳定参考。基于此，通过源自寄存器注意力的动态阈值，执行图像自适应冗余剪枝和查询自适应相关性剪枝。

Result: 在LLaVA-NeXT、LLaVA-v1.5和Qwen3-VL上进行了实验，一致改善了准确率与效率的权衡。在LLaVA-NeXT上，将2880个视觉Token压缩至约40个（保留率仅1.4%），同时保持了超过93%的原始准确率。

Conclusion: OccamToken证明了基于寄存器的相对证据测试在视觉Token剪枝中的有效性，成功解决了绝对排序范式的脆弱性问题，在极端压缩比下仍能实现稳定的性能表现。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) rely on long visual token sequences for visual understanding, making the prefill stage expensive in both computation and memory. Most existing pruning methods follow an absolute-ranking paradigm, assigning importance scores to visual tokens and retaining a fixed top-K subset. In this work, we argue that this paradigm is fundamentally brittle: attention sinks distort token importance rankings, while image redundancy and query-dependent visual evidence make fixed token budgets unreliable across inputs. We propose OccamToken, a training-free framework that replaces absolute token ranking with register-anchored relative evidence testing. Instead of asking which tokens are globally important, OccamToken evaluates whether a visual token provides information beyond a register-based reference. Our key insight is that register tokens naturally absorb low-information attention patterns, making them a stable reference for identifying genuinely informative visual evidence. Based on this principle, OccamToken performs both image-adaptive redundancy pruning and query-adaptive relevance pruning through dynamic thresholds derived from register attention. Across LLaVA-NeXT, LLaVA-v1.5, and Qwen3-VL, OccamToken consistently improves the accuracy-efficiency trade-off without additional training. Notably, on LLaVA-NeXT, it reduces 2,880 visual tokens to approximately 40 while preserving over 93% of the original accuracy, enabling stable visual token compression even in the extreme 1.4% retention regime.

</details>


### [29] [Geometry-Guided Modeling of Foundation Features Enables Generalizable Object Shape Deformation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29661)
*Yiyao Ma,Kai Chen,Zhongxiang Zhou,Zhuheng Song,Dongsheng Xie,Zelong Tan,Rong Xiong,Qi Dou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个通用的变形学习框架，通过显式变形类别级形状模板来重建3D物体，在处理大形状变化和多样视角方面显著优于现有方法，并展现出对新颖类别的强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目3D形状恢复对几何理解至关重要，但在任意视角和未见物体类别上实现稳健泛化仍然是一个重大挑战。

Method: 1) 提出一个通用变形学习框架，通过显式变形类别级形状模板来匹配目标观测；2) 引入几何引导的特征建模机制，用模板拓扑丰富基础特征生成几何感知表示，并与目标观测显式关联以引导精确变形；3) 提出视角自适应特征聚合模块，利用多视图模板特征及其相机姿态来丰富规范模板表示，确保无论目标视角如何都能实现稳健的特征对齐。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在处理大形状变化和多样视角方面显著优于最先进方法，对新颖类别展现出强泛化能力，并有效支持下游现实世界的灵巧机器人操作任务。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了单目3D形状恢复中跨视角和未见物体类别的泛化挑战，并在机器人操作等实际应用中展现出良好的实用性。

Abstract: Monocular 3D shape recovery is fundamental to geometric understanding, yet achieving robust generalization across arbitrary viewpoints and unseen object categories remains a significant challenge. In this paper, we present a generalizable deformation learning framework that reconstructs 3D objects by explicitly deforming a category-level shape template to match the target observation. To address complex shape variations between the template and the target, we introduce a geometry-guided feature modeling mechanism. This process first enriches foundation features with template topology to yield a geometry-aware representation, which is then explicitly correlated with the target observation to guide precise deformation. Furthermore, to bridge the disparity between the fixed template and arbitrary target views, we propose a view-adaptive feature aggregation module. This module leverages multi-view template features and their corresponding camera poses to enrich the canonical template representation, ensuring robust feature alignment regardless of the target's perspective. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in handling large shape variations and diverse viewpoints, exhibiting strong generalization to novel categories and effectively supporting downstream real-world dexterous robotic manipulation tasks. Project homepage: https://GODeform.github.io/

</details>


### [30] [SAFE-Pruner: Semantic Attention-Guided Future-Aware Token Pruning for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29662)
*Shilin Ma,Chubin Zhang,Changyuan Wang,Yuji Wang,Yue Wu,Zixuan Wang,Jingqi Tian,Zheng Zhu,Yansong Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SAFE-Pruner，一种即插即用的视觉token剪枝框架，通过利用未来层的注意力线索来预测深层token显著性，实现了VLA模型的高效推理加速，在保持成功率的同时达到1.89倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型的实时推理对机器人控制至关重要。现有的视觉token剪枝方法主要依赖浅层线索进行剪枝决策，存在过早丢弃深层所需视觉信息的风险，影响模型性能和稳定性。

Method: 提出SAFE-Pruner框架：1)发现并利用"语义注意力一致性"现象，即VLA模型在执行步骤中持续关注相同语义实体；2)设计前瞻策略预测深层token显著性，防止关键token被过早移除；3)引入自适应子任务划分策略检测注意力突变，提高预测准确性和剪枝可靠性。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境实验中，该方法实现了高达1.89倍推理加速，成功率下降幅度小于1.7%，相比最先进方法性能提升达1.9%。

Conclusion: 通过将未来层注意力线索纳入剪枝决策，SAFE-Pruner成功解决了现有方法过早丢弃关键视觉信息的问题，在加速推理的同时保持了模型性能，为VLA模型的实时部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Real-time inference of vision-language-action (VLA) models is essential for robotic control. While visual token pruning has shown strong potential for accelerating inference, most existing methods mainly base pruning decisions on shallow-layer cues and risk discarding visual information required by deep layers. To address this issue, we propose SAFE-Pruner, a plug-and-play pruning framework that incorporates attention cues of future layers into pruning decisions. Specifically, we identify semantic attention consistency, the tendency that VLA models concentrate their attention probability mass on the same semantic entity across execution steps. Based on this observation, we design a forward-looking strategy to forecast the token saliency in deep layers, which prevents the premature removal of critical tokens and leads to more stable acceleration. We further introduce an adaptive subtask division strategy to detect abrupt attention shifts, thereby improving forecasting accuracy and pruning reliability. Extensive experiments in simulation and real-world settings demonstrate that our method achieves up to 1.89x speedup with a minimal degradation in success rate of less than 1.7%, while outperforming state-of-the-art methods by up to 1.9%.

</details>


### [31] [Unsupervised Semantic Segmentation Facilitates Model Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29691)
*Xiaoyan Yu,Lisa Mais,Jannik Franzen,Peter Hirsch,Nick Lechtenbörger,Andreas Mardt,Dagmar Kainmüller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简单可解释的可视化协议，基于无监督语义分割结果来展示自监督学习视觉transformers的行为特征，揭示了不同模型的位置偏差、缩放行为以及DINOv3-Large中的边界伪影，并能清晰区分位置效应与局部性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究揭示了对比学习(CL)和掩码图像建模(MIM)训练的模型之间存在显著差异，但这些理解尚未充分渗透到更广泛的社区中，CL模型的见解有时被错误地推广到MIM模型。因此需要一种直观易懂的方法来帮助广泛受众理解模型行为。

Method: 提出基于无监督语义分割结果可视化的简单可解释协议。该协议的目标不是最大化分割性能，而是展示模型在多张图像间一致出现的行为特征。通过对多样化的SSL模型在不同层和表示上进行基准测试。

Result: 获得了关于不同位置偏差和缩放行为的新见解，发现了DINOv3-Large模型标记中存在强烈的边界伪影。该协议能够清晰区分位置效应与局部性偏差（后者在文献中研究更为广泛）。这些发现补充并帮助传达了以往的研究结果。

Conclusion: 该可视化协议已在GitHub上公开，预期能够促进广泛社区对模型的进一步理解，使模型行为特征的传达变得直观且易于理解。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) has produced a diverse landscape of vision transformers (ViTs) whose pretrained representations support a wide range of downstream tasks. Towards a better understanding of these models, a body of work has assessed the mechanics of their self-attention as well as the types of information captured across their representations, revealing, for example, stark differences between models trained with contrastive learning (CL) and masked image modeling (MIM). However, these advances in model understanding have not yet fully permeated the broader community, where insights specific to CL models are sometimes generalized to MIM models. To make model understanding straightforward and intuitive for a broad audience, we propose a simple and easily interpretable visualization protocol.
  Our protocol is based on visualizing unsupervised semantic segmentation results, yet our goal is not to maximize segmentation performance. Instead, it allows us to convey model behaviors that consistently emerge across images. Benchmarking a diverse set of SSL models across layers and representations, we obtain novel insights into distinct positional biases and scaling behaviors, including strong boundary artifacts in DINOv3-Large model tokens. These insights complement and help communicate a range of previous findings.
  Our protocol further enables a clear visual distinction between positional effects and the closely related but distinct locality bias, which has been studied much more extensively in the literature. The protocol is publicly available on GitHub and we believe it will catalyze further model understanding for a broad community.

</details>


### [32] [S2MDF: A Plug-And-Play Layer for Intersection-Free Multi-Object Signed Distance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29761)
*Deniz Sayin Mercadier,Federico Stella,Aurel Bizeau,Nicolas Talabot,Pascal Fua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出S2MDF模块，通过硬约束解决组合式SDF表示中的物体穿透问题，在保证重建质量的同时将穿透降至数值精度级别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合式隐式表面表示将场景建模为多个物体的集合，每个物体用SDF编码。现有方法的根本局限是多个SDF可能产生相互穿透的几何体，违反物理合理性。现有的缓解策略依赖软惩罚项，只能减少但不能消除穿透，且需要仔细调整损失权重。

Method: 提出对向量值SDF的硬约束，并引入S2MDF——一个轻量级即插即用模块，无需架构修改即可在任何物体组合式SDF表示上强制执行该约束。该模块计算开销可忽略，兼容Marching Cubes等线性插值标准网格化算法，可在训练时应用或作为后处理步骤。

Result: 在多个最先进组合方法上的实验表明，S2MDF将穿透减少至数值精度级别，同时保持重建质量，性能优于现有缓解策略。

Conclusion: S2MDF成功解决了组合式SDF表示中的物体穿透问题，在不牺牲重建质量和计算效率的前提下实现了物理合理的几何表示，具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Compositional implicit surface representations model scenes as collections of objects, each encoded by a Signed Distance Field (SDF). A fundamental limitation of this approach is that multiple SDFs can produce geometries that interpenetrate, violating physical plausibility. Existing mitigation strategies rely on soft penalty terms that reduce but do not eliminate intersections, and require careful loss weighting. To truly prevent interpenetration, we propose a hard constraint on vector-valued SDFs and introduce S2MDF, a lightweight plug-and-play module that enforces the constraint on any object-compositional SDF representation without architectural modifications. It introduces negligible computational overhead and is compatible with linearly-interpolated standard meshing algorithms such as Marching Cubes. It can be applied during training or as a post-processing step. Experiments on multiple state-of-the-art compositional methods show that S2MDF reduces intersections to numerical precision while preserving reconstruction quality, outperforming existing mitigation strategies.

</details>


### [33] [GeoMag: Geometric-Aware Video Motion Magnification via State Space Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29762)
*Kecheng Han,Yuchen Zhang,Bingqing Liu,Boqiang Guo,Wenbin Zheng,Shiyuan Pei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoMag框架，基于状态空间模型实现几何感知的视频运动放大，并构建Geo-200K大规模合成数据集，解决了现有方法在复杂几何变换下的结构不一致性和计算效率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频运动放大方法在复杂几何变换下存在结构不一致性问题；学习型方法面临CNN全局上下文受限与Transformers高计算成本的权衡；当前训练协议主要由简单线性运动主导，无法捕获真实世界视频中的几何和成像复杂性。

Method: 提出GeoMag几何感知VMM框架，基于状态空间模型实现线性复杂度的全局一致运动放大；构建Geo-200K大规模合成数据集，引入丰富几何变换和传感器真实退化，提升训练信号的多样性和真实性。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准测试上的大量实验表明，GeoMag在视觉保真度和计算效率方面始终优于先前方法，产生更少伪影且具有更好的结构一致性。

Conclusion: GeoMag通过状态空间模型实现了高效且全局一致的运动放大，配合Geo-200K数据集的训练策略，有效解决了VMM领域的结构一致性、计算效率和训练数据多样性等关键问题。

Abstract: Video Motion Magnification (VMM) reveals imperceptible dynamics but often suffers from structural inconsistencies under complex geometric transformations. Existing learning-based methods generally face a trade-off between the limited global context of CNNs and the high computational cost of Transformers. In addition, current training protocols, largely dominated by simple linear motion, fail to capture the geometric and imaging complexities encountered in real-world videos. To address these issues, we propose GeoMag, a geometric-aware VMM framework built upon State Space Models to achieve globally consistent motion amplification with linear complexity. We further construct Geo-200K, a large-scale synthetic dataset that introduces rich geometric transformations together with sensor-realistic degradations, improving the diversity and realism of training signals. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks show that GeoMag consistently outperforms prior methods in visual fidelity and computational efficiency, while producing fewer artifacts and better structural consistency.

</details>


### [34] [Energy-Aware NECO for Single-Pass Pixel-wise Out-of-Distribution Detection in Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29773)
*Boyuan Zhang,Huanshan Huang,Yifei Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Energy-Aware NECO，一种单次前向传播的像素级分布外(OOD)检测方法，通过融合NECO几何比率与能量分数实现，在miniMUAD数据集上达到0.8539 AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人需要可靠的语义分割系统，不仅要求精确的密集预测，还需在分布偏移下具备鲁棒的不确定性估计。现有的强不确定性基线方法（如蒙特卡洛Dropout）通常需要重复的随机前向传播，难以在边缘平台上高效部署。

Method: 提出Energy-Aware NECO单次前向传播像素级OOD检测器，包含两个核心组件：(1)从解码器特征计算的中心化NECO风格几何比率；(2)基于logit的能量分数。两个组件均使用纯分布内验证集统计量进行标准化，并通过凸组合进行融合。

Result: 在miniMUAD子集上使用真实像素级OOD标签进行评估。提出的混合分数达到0.8539 AUROC，优于单独使用NECO(0.8280)、单独使用Energy(0.8171)以及集成预测熵基线(0.8124)。定性和操作点分析显示混合检测器改善了整体排序性能。

Conclusion: 混合检测器在保持单次前向传播设计的高效率优势的同时，显著提升了整体排序性能，为移动机器人语义分割中的OOD检测提供了高效且准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable semantic segmentation for mobile robots requires both accurate dense prediction and robust uncertainty estimation under distribution shift. Strong uncertainty baselines such as Monte Carlo Dropout often require repeated stochastic forward passes and are difficult to deploy on edge platforms.
  We propose Energy-Aware NECO, a single-pass pixel-wise out-of-distribution (OOD) detector for semantic segmentation. The method combines a centered NECO-style geometric ratio computed from decoder features with a logit-based Energy score. Both components are standardized using statistics fitted on a pure in-distribution validation split and fused through a convex combination.
  We evaluate the method on the miniMUAD subset using true pixel-level OOD labels. The proposed hybrid score achieves an AUROC of 0.8539, outperforming NECO-only (0.8280), Energy-only (0.8171), and an ensemble predictive-entropy baseline (0.8124). Additional qualitative and operating-point analyses show that the hybrid detector improves overall ranking performance while preserving the efficiency advantages of a single-pass design.
  Code is available at https://github.com/boyuan-zhangx/Energy-Aware_NECO

</details>


### [35] [Improving CLIP Adaptation by Breaking Tail Alignment for Source-Free Cross-Domain Few-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29776)
*Shuai Yi,Yixiong Zou,Yuhua Li,Ruixuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了ATHA（自适应尾部-头部对齐），一种针对CLIP在跨域少样本学习中的新型微调策略，通过自适应地加强或减弱图像token与文本嵌入的对齐，显著提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP等视觉-语言模型虽然具有强大的零样本泛化能力，但在目标域训练数据稀缺的跨域场景（CDFSL）中性能显著下降。现有微调方法对所有图像patch token与文本嵌入进行统一对齐，但作者发现一个反直觉现象：主动将某些低相似度的"尾部token"推离文本嵌入反而能一致提升目标域性能。

Method: 提出ATHA方法，将传统的统一对齐范式转变为自适应对齐范式。核心思想是：在大域迁移和稀缺训练数据下，模型难以从视觉输入中提取语义信息，因此对齐仅对已包含足够语义信息的token有效；对于尾部token，强制对齐会导致对稀缺训练数据的过度拟合，而打破对齐更为有用。ATHA实现了对齐的增强和弱化的自适应调整。

Result: 在四个具有挑战性的CDFSL基准测试上进行了大量实验，验证了该方法达到了最先进的性能水平。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在跨域少样本学习场景中，传统的"所有token都应与文本嵌入对齐"的假设并不总是成立。对于包含足够语义信息的token应加强对其，而对于低相似度的尾部token应打破对齐以避免过拟合。这种自适应对齐策略为CLIP在跨域场景的微调提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) such as CLIP demonstrate strong zero-shot generalization, but their performance significantly degrades in cross-domain scenarios with scarce target-domain training data (Cross-Domain Few-Shot Learning, CDFSL). In this paper, we focus on the target-domain few-shot finetuning in the CLIP-based CDFSL task. Prevailing finetuning paradigms uniformly align all image patch tokens with their corresponding textual embeddings. However, we find a counterintuitive phenomenon: actively pushing away certain low-similarity image tokens, termed "tail tokens", from their textual embeddings consistently improves target-domain performance. We delve into this phenomenon and provide a novel interpretation: under great domain shifts and scarce training data, the model can hardly extract semantic information from visual inputs; therefore, the common belief of alignment is valid only for tokens already containing sufficient semantic information; for tail tokens, forcing the alignment would lead to excessive overfitting to the scarce training, while breaking the alignment is more useful. Motivated by this, we propose Adaptive Tail-Head Alignment (ATHA), a novel fine-tuning strategy for CLIP that transforms the conventional uniform alignment paradigm to an adaptive alignment paradigm, with both alignment strengthening and weakening. Extensive experiments on four challenging CDFSL benchmarks validate our state-of-the-art performance. Our code is available at https://github.com/shuaiyi308/ATHA.

</details>


### [36] [Fewer Steps, Better Performance: Efficient Cross-Modal Clip Trimming for Video Moment Retrieval Using Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29793)
*Xiang Fang,Daizong Liu,Wanlong Fang,Pan Zhou,Zichuan Xu,Wenzheng Xu,Junyang Chen,Renfu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SpotVMR，一种高效的即插即用式视频片段检索方法，通过学习识别查询相关的视频区域来提升检索效率，同时保持良好的检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频时刻检索方法需要对未裁剪视频进行稀疏下采样为固定长度的视频片段，这种方法对于长达数小时的长视频不可行。下采样会过滤掉查询相关的帧，导致目标时刻边界模糊、跨模态不对齐，并引入边界偏差和推理偏差。

Method: 提出SpotVMR方法：(1)设计新颖的片段搜索模型，根据语言查询学习识别有前景的视频区域；(2)引入低成本语义索引特征来捕获对象和交互的上下文信息；(3)利用蒸馏损失解决片段选择器和VMR模型端到端联合训练的优化问题。该方法可作为即插即用模块集成到现有VMR方法中。

Result: 在三个挑战性数据集上的广泛实验证明了该方法的有效性。SpotVMR能够在保持良好检索性能的同时，为最先进的VMR方法提供效率提升。

Conclusion: SpotVMR成功解决了现有VMR方法在长视频处理上的效率和精度问题，通过智能定位查询相关区域实现了高效检索，具有良好的实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Given an untrimmed video and a sentence query, video moment retrieval using language (VMR) aims to locate a target query-relevant moment. Since the untrimmed video is overlong, almost all existing VMR methods first sparsely down-sample each untrimmed video into multiple fixed-length video clips and then conduct multi-modal interactions with the query feature and expensive clip features for reasoning, which is infeasible for long real-world videos that span hours. Since the video is downsampled into fixed-length clips, some query-related frames may be filtered out, which will blur the specific boundary of the target moment, take the adjacent irrelevant frames as new boundaries, easily leading to cross-modal misalignment and introducing both boundary-bias and reasoning-bias. To this end, in this paper, we propose an efficient approach, SpotVMR, to trim the query-relevant clip. Besides, our proposed SpotVMR can serve as plug-and-play module, which achieves efficiency for state-of-the-art VMR methods while maintaining good retrieval performance. Especially, we first design a novel clip search model that learns to identify promising video regions to search conditioned on the language query. Then, we introduce a set of low-cost semantic indexing features to capture the context of objects and interactions that suggest where to search the query-relevant moment. Also, the distillation loss is utilized to address the optimization issues arising from end-to-end joint training of the clip selector and VMR model. Extensive experiments on three challenging datasets demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [37] [Low-Magnification SEM May Suffice: Interpretable Deep Learning for Multi-Scale Fracture-Cause Classification in Zirconia-Toughened Alumina](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29798)
*Julian Schmid,Pawel Astankow,Tom Vater,Julius Beck,Robert Cichon,Danny Krautz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可解释的视觉变换器（ViT）工作流程，用于自动分类陶瓷髋膝关节植入物中的断裂原因，在低倍率条件下达到了0.907的准确率和0.888的宏观F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前氧化铝基复合材料髋膝关节植入物的断裂源识别对质量保证和患者安全至关重要，但现有的断口分析流程耗时、部分主观，且依赖于高倍扫描电子显微镜（SEM）。需要开发自动化、可靠的识别方法以提高效率并减少对高倍镜检查的依赖。

Method: 构建了包含8,493张SEM图像（50x-10,000x）的数据集，来源于五年生产过程中的爆破和验证测试，标注为三个缺陷类别：生坯、硬加工和材料缺陷。采用可解释的视觉变换器（ViT）进行微调，使用分层五折交叉验证，并通过两阶段感知哈希/SSIM泄露审计确认样本重叠可忽略。使用Grad-CAM进行可解释性分析。

Result: 微调后的ViT在严重类别不平衡情况下达到0.907准确率和0.888宏观F1分数。关键发现是低倍率（50x）性能与高倍率（1k-10kx）相当，表明宏观尺度特征（镜面几何和羽毛线场）已编码足够的诊断信号。Grad-CAM归因一致地定位在典型的断口学线索上（镜面、羽毛线、孔隙、加工痕迹），与既定的断口学标准一致。

Conclusion: 可解释的ViT可作为陶瓷植入物质量保证的补充工具，实现低倍率预筛选，减少对耗时的高倍率检查的依赖，为临床植入物的质量管控提供了新的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable identification of fracture origins in alumina matrix composite hip and knee implants is critical for quality assurance and patient safety, yet current fractographic workflows are time-consuming, partly subjective, and reliant on high-magnification scanning electron microscopy (SEM). We present an interpretable vision-transformer (ViT) workflow for automated classification of fracture causes in an alumina matrix composite (BIOLOX delta, CeramTec GmbH) widely used in total joint replacements. A dataset of 8,493 SEM images (50x-10,000x) was curated from five years of in-production burst and proof tests and annotated into three defect categories defined along the manufacturing chain: green body, hard machining, and material defects. Under severe class imbalance, the fine-tuned ViT reached an accuracy of 0.907 and a macro-F1 of 0.888 in stratified five-fold cross-validation, with a two-stage perceptual-hash/SSIM leakage audit confirming negligible specimen overlap. Notably, performance at low magnification (50x) was comparable to that at high magnification (1k-10kx), indicating that macro-scale features - mirror geometry and hackle line fields - already encode sufficient diagnostic signal.
